第九轮：量子认知与神经科学基础——决策的量子大脑

9.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：人类决策并非经典概率下的贝叶斯更新，而是量子概率（Quantum Probability）的体现。这解释了为什么人类会出现： - 违反确定性公理（Sure Thing Principle的违反） - 语境依赖性（Contextuality） - 顺序效应（Question Order Effects）

数学重构： 将决策状态表示为密度矩阵 ，而非经典概率向量。观测（提问/情境）对应POVM测量（Positive Operator-Valued Measure）。

其中  是测量算符，且 。

9.2 代码实现：量子认知决策引擎

# quantum_cognition_engine.py
# 量子认知引擎：人类决策的量子概率模型

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

@dataclass
class QuantumMentalState:
    """量子心智状态：密度矩阵表示"""
    density_matrix: np.ndarray  # 2^n x 2^n 密度矩阵（n是信念维度）
    entanglement_correlations: Dict[Tuple[str, str], float]  # 信念间的量子纠缠
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        # 验证密度矩阵性质：厄米、半正定、迹为1
        assert np.allclose(self.density_matrix, self.density_matrix.conj().T), "密度矩阵必须厄米"
        assert np.all(np.linalg.eigvals(self.density_matrix) >= -1e-10), "密度矩阵必须半正定"
        assert np.isclose(np.trace(self.density_matrix), 1.0), "密度矩阵迹必须为1"

class QuantumDecisionOperator:
    """量子决策算符：测量不同决策基"""
    
    def __init__(self, basis_states: Dict[str, np.ndarray]):
        """
        basis_states: 决策基的态矢量，如 {'accept': [1,0], 'reject': [0,1]}
        """
        self.bases = basis_states
        self.povm_operators = self._construct_povm()
    
    def _construct_povm(self) -> Dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        """构建POVM测量算符（满足完备性条件）"""
        operators = {}
        total = np.zeros((2, 2))
        
        for name, state in self.bases.items():
            # 投影算符 |ψ><ψ|
            proj = np.outer(state, state.conj())
            operators[name] = proj
            total += proj
        
        # 验证完备性：sum(M_a) = I
        assert np.allclose(total, np.eye(2)), "POVM算符必须完备"
        
        return operators
    
    def measure(self, 
                mental_state: QuantumMentalState,
                context: str = 'default') -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        量子测量：Born规则计算概率
        
        P(a) = Tr(ρ * M_a)
        """
        probabilities = {}
        
        for decision, operator in self.povm_operators.items():
            # Born规则：概率 = Tr(ρM)
            prob = np.trace(mental_state.density_matrix @ operator).real
            probabilities[decision] = max(0, min(1, prob))  # 数值稳定性裁剪
        
        # 归一化（处理数值误差）
        total = sum(probabilities.values())
        if total > 0:
            probabilities = {k: v/total for k, v in probabilities.items()}
        
        return probabilities

class ContextualityDetector:
    """语境性检测器：检测决策是否违反经典概率（证明量子性）"""
    
    def detect_violation(self, 
                         probabilities: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]) -> bool:
        """
        检测CHSH不等式违反或类似量子语境性
        
        经典概率满足：P(a|A) = sum_b P(a,b|A,B) （无信号性）
        量子概率可能违反此约束（语境依赖性）
        """
        # 简化检测：检查是否违反确定性公理
        # 如果 P(接受|情境A) + P(拒绝|情境A) > 1（考虑误差），则可能是量子叠加
        
        violations = []
        
        for context, probs in probabilities.items():
            total = sum(probs.values())
            if not np.isclose(total, 1.0, atol=0.01):
                violations.append({
                    'context': context,
                    'probability_sum': total,
                    'violation_type': 'probability_nonconservation'
                })
        
        return len(violations) > 0, violations

class NeuralQuantumInterface:
    """神经-量子接口：从神经信号推断量子态"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        # 神经编码器：将fMRI/EEG信号映射到密度矩阵参数
        self.neural_encoder = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(128, 256),  # 输入：神经特征
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(256, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Linear(128, 4)  # 输出：密度矩阵的4个实参数（2x2厄米矩阵）
        )
    
    def neural_to_density_matrix(self, 
                                  neural_features: torch.Tensor) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        从神经特征重建密度矩阵
        
        2x2厄米矩阵参数化：
        ρ = [[a+b, c+di],
             [c-di, a-b]] / 2
        其中 a=1（归一化），b,c,d ∈ [-1,1]
        """
        params = self.neural_encoder(neural_features).detach().numpy()
        b, c, d = params[0], params[1], params[2]
        
        # 构建密度矩阵
        rho = np.array([
            [1 + b, c + 1j*d],
            [c - 1j*d, 1 - b]
        ]) / 2.0
        
        # 确保物理有效性
        rho = self._ensure_physical(rho)
        
        return rho
    
    def _ensure_physical(self, rho: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """确保密度矩阵物理有效（半正定、迹1）"""
        # 对角化并投影到物理子空间
        eigenvalues, eigenvectors = np.linalg.eigh(rho)
        eigenvalues = np.clip(eigenvalues, 0, 1)  # 半正定投影
        eigenvalues = eigenvalues / np.sum(eigenvalues)  # 归一化
        
        # 重建
        rho_physical = eigenvectors @ np.diag(eigenvalues) @ eigenvectors.conj().T
        return rho_physical

class QuantumCognitionDiagnostics:
    """量子认知诊断：识别创始人决策的量子特征"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.operators = {
            'risk_acceptance': QuantumDecisionOperator({
                'accept': np.array([np.cos(np.pi/8), np.sin(np.pi/8)]),
                'reject': np.array([-np.sin(np.pi/8), np.cos(np.pi/8)])
            }),
            'trust_assessment': QuantumDecisionOperator({
                'high_trust': np.array([1, 0]),
                'low_trust': np.array([0, 1])
            })
        }
    
    def diagnose_decision_pattern(self,
                                   historical_decisions: List[Dict],
                                   neural_signals: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        诊断决策模式：经典vs量子
        
        返回：
        - 量子性指标（0=完全经典，1=完全量子）
        - 纠缠强度（不同决策维度的关联）
        - 干涉模式（顺序效应强度）
        """
        # 构建密度矩阵（从行为数据估计）
        if neural_signals is not None:
            neural_interface = NeuralQuantumInterface()
            rho = neural_interface.neural_to_density_matrix(
                torch.tensor(neural_signals)
            )
        else:
            # 从行为数据最大似然估计
            rho = self._estimate_density_matrix_from_behavior(historical_decisions)
        
        mental_state = QuantumMentalState(rho, {})
        
        # 测量不同情境下的概率
        probabilities = {}
        for context_name, operator in self.operators.items():
            probs = operator.measure(mental_state, context_name)
            probabilities[context_name] = probs
        
        # 检测语境性违反
        is_quantum, violations = ContextualityDetector().detect_violation(probabilities)
        
        # 计算纠缠熵（冯·诺依曼熵）
        eigenvalues = np.linalg.eigvalsh(rho)
        entropy = -np.sum(eigenvalues * np.log2(eigenvalues + 1e-10))
        
        return {
            'quantumness_index': float(entropy / np.log2(len(eigenvalues))),  # 归一化熵
            'is_likely_quantum': is_quantum,
            'violations': violations,
            'density_matrix': rho,
            'interference_strength': self._calculate_interference(rho),
            'decision_probabilities': probabilities
        }
    
    def _estimate_density_matrix_from_behavior(self, 
                                               decisions: List[Dict]) -> np.ndarray:
        """从行为数据估计密度矩阵（最大熵估计）"""
        # 简化：假设二元决策，估计布洛赫球坐标
        n_accept = sum(1 for d in decisions if d['choice'] == 'accept')
        n_total = len(decisions)
        p_accept = n_accept / n_total
        
        # 布洛赫球参数
        theta = 2 * np.arccos(np.sqrt(p_accept))
        phi = 0  # 假设无相位（简化）
        
        # 构建纯态密度矩阵
        psi = np.array([np.cos(theta/2), np.sin(theta/2) * np.exp(1j*phi)])
        rho = np.outer(psi, psi.conj())
        
        # 添加退相干（混合态）
        mixing = 0.1  # 退相干程度
        rho_mixed = (1-mixing) * rho + mixing * np.eye(2) / 2
        
        return rho_mixed
    
    def _calculate_interference(self, rho: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """计算干涉项强度（量子相干）"""
        # 非对角元幅度表示量子相干
        coherence = np.abs(rho[0, 1])
        return float(coherence)

# 验证代码
def test_quantum_cognition():
    """测试量子认知引擎"""
    print("=== 量子认知验证 ===")
    
    # 1. 测试密度矩阵构建
    rho = np.array([[0.7, 0.2+0.1j], [0.2-0.1j, 0.3]])
    state = QuantumMentalState(rho, {})
    print(f"密度矩阵迹: {np.trace(rho):.2f} (应为1)")
    assert np.isclose(np.trace(rho), 1.0)
    
    # 2. 测试量子决策算符
    operator = QuantumDecisionOperator({
        'yes': np.array([1, 0]),
        'no': np.array([0, 1])
    })
    probs = operator.measure(state)
    print(f"决策概率: {probs}")
    assert np.isclose(sum(probs.values()), 1.0)
    
    # 3. 测试诊断系统
    diagnostics = QuantumCognitionDiagnostics()
    mock_decisions = [
        {'choice': 'accept', 'context': 'low_risk'},
        {'choice': 'reject', 'context': 'high_risk'},
        {'choice': 'accept', 'context': 'low_risk'}
    ]
    
    result = diagnostics.diagnose_decision_pattern(mock_decisions)
    print(f"\n量子性指标: {result['quantumness_index']:.2f}")
    print(f"干涉强度: {result['interference_strength']:.2f}")
    print(f"是否可能量子: {result['is_likely_quantum']}")
    
    print("✓ 量子认知验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_quantum_cognition()

第十轮：复杂网络与信任传染——生态级联效应

10.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：合伙人信任崩塌不是孤立事件，而是创业生态系统中的级联失效（Cascading Failure）。一个创始人的信任危机会通过网络传染影响： - 共同投资人（信号传递） - 上下游合作伙伴（信心动摇） - 行业人才池（声誉效应）

数学重构： 使用渗流理论（Percolation Theory）和SIR模型的变体——信任传染模型（Trust Contagion Model）。

10.2 代码实现：网络信任动力学

# network_trust_contagion.py
# 网络信任传染：复杂网络中的级联效应

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
import networkx as nx
from collections import deque

@dataclass
class FounderNode:
    """创始人节点：网络中的顶点"""
    node_id: str
    trust_state: float  # 0-1，信任健康度
    susceptibility: float  # 易感性（对负面信号的敏感程度）
    influence_score: float  # 影响力（PageRank-like）
    sector: str  # 所属行业
    stage: str   # 融资阶段

class TrustContagionNetwork:
    """信任传染网络：渗流理论实现"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.graph = nx.DiGraph()  # 有向图（影响力方向）
        self.nodes: Dict[str, FounderNode] = {}
        
        # SIR模型参数（信任专用）
        self.beta = 0.3  # 传染率（负面信任事件的传播概率）
        self.gamma = 0.1  # 恢复率（信任修复速度）
        self.mu = 0.05   # 自然衰减率
        
    def add_founder(self, founder: FounderNode):
        """添加创始人节点"""
        self.nodes[founder.node_id] = founder
        self.graph.add_node(founder.node_id, data=founder)
    
    def add_influence_edge(self, 
                          from_id: str, 
                          to_id: str, 
                          weight: float,
                          edge_type: str = 'investor_overlap'):
        """
        添加影响边
        
        edge_type: 影响类型
        - investor_overlap: 共同投资人（强影响）
        - sector_peers: 同行（中等影响）
        - media_attention: 媒体关注（弱但广泛影响）
        """
        self.graph.add_edge(from_id, to_id, 
                           weight=weight, 
                           type=edge_type)
    
    def simulate_cascade(self, 
                         initial_failing_node: str,
                         steps: int = 50) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
        """
        模拟级联失效：信任崩塌的传染过程
        
        状态：S(信任健康) -> I(信任危机) -> R(信任死亡/重组)
        """
        # 初始化
        states = {nid: 'S' for nid in self.nodes}
        states[initial_failing_node] = 'I'
        
        # 记录轨迹
        trajectories = {nid: [self.nodes[nid].trust_state] for nid in self.nodes}
        
        for step in range(steps):
            new_states = states.copy()
            
            for node_id, state in states.items():
                if state == 'I':  # 信任危机中
                    # 1. 尝试恢复（gamma概率）
                    if np.random.random() < self.gamma:
                        new_states[node_id] = 'R'  # 恢复或死亡
                    
                    # 2. 传染邻居（beta概率 * 边权重 * 影响力）
                    for successor in self.graph.successors(node_id):
                        if states[successor] == 'S':
                            edge_weight = self.graph[node_id][successor]['weight']
                            infection_prob = (self.beta * 
                                           edge_weight * 
                                           self.nodes[node_id].influence_score)
                            
                            if np.random.random() < infection_prob:
                                new_states[successor] = 'I'
                                # 降低信任状态
                                self.nodes[successor].trust_state *= 0.7
                
                elif state == 'S':  # 健康但可能自然衰减
                    if np.random.random() < self.mu:
                        self.nodes[node_id].trust_state *= 0.99
            
            states = new_states
            
            # 记录当前状态
            for nid in self.nodes:
                trajectories[nid].append(self.nodes[nid].trust_state)
        
        return trajectories
    
    def calculate_systemic_risk(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        计算系统性风险：网络整体的脆弱性
        
        指标：
        1.  giant_component_size: 最大连通分量大小（崩塌传播范围）
        2.  average_path_length: 平均路径长度（传染速度）
        3.  clustering_coefficient: 聚类系数（局部传染强度）
        """
        # 转换为无向图计算连通性
        undirected = self.graph.to_undirected()
        
        if nx.is_connected(undirected):
            avg_path = nx.average_shortest_path_length(undirected)
        else:
            avg_path = float('inf')
        
        # 最大连通分量
        giant_component = max(nx.connected_components(undirected), key=len)
        
        return {
            'giant_component_ratio': len(giant_component) / len(self.nodes),
            'average_path_length': avg_path,
            'clustering_coefficient': nx.average_clustering(undirected),
            'network_density': nx.density(self.graph),
            'systemic_risk_score': self._compute_risk_score(undirected)
        }
    
    def _compute_risk_score(self, graph) -> float:
        """计算综合风险分数（0-1）"""
        # 高连通性 + 高聚类 = 高风险（级联容易传播）
        density = nx.density(graph)
        clustering = nx.average_clustering(graph)
        
        # 加权组合
        risk = 0.6 * density + 0.4 * clustering
        return min(1.0, risk)
    
    def identify_super_spreaders(self, 
                                  top_k: int = 5) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
        """识别超级传播者：高影响力且高易感度的节点"""
        scores = {}
        
        for node_id, node in self.nodes.items():
            # 影响力（PageRank）
            pagerank = nx.pagerank(self.graph).get(node_id, 0)
            # 易感性（反向抵抗力）
            susceptibility = node.susceptibility
            
            # 风险分数：高影响力 + 高易感性 = 潜在超级传播者
            scores[node_id] = pagerank * susceptibility
        
        return sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:top_k]

class ImmunizationStrategy:
    """免疫策略：如何防止信任传染"""
    
    def __init__(self, network: TrustContagionNetwork):
        self.network = network
    
    def calculate_optimal_immunization(self, 
                                       budget: int,
                                       strategy: str = 'targeted') -> List[str]:
        """
        计算最优免疫策略
        
        strategy:
        - random: 随机免疫（基准）
        - targeted: 目标免疫（免疫高连接节点）
        - acquaintance: 熟人免疫（随机选邻居，利用友谊悖论）
        """
        if strategy == 'random':
            return np.random.choice(list(self.network.nodes.keys()), 
                                   size=budget, replace=False).tolist()
        
        elif strategy == 'targeted':
            # 免疫度最高的节点（枢纽）
            degrees = dict(self.network.graph.degree())
            return sorted(degrees.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], 
                         reverse=True)[:budget]
        
        elif strategy == 'acquaintance':
            # 熟人免疫：随机选边，免疫远端节点
            edges = list(self.network.graph.edges())
            selected = set()
            while len(selected) < budget and edges:
                edge = edges[np.random.randint(len(edges))]
                # 选边的远端（更可能是枢纽，友谊悖论）
                if self.network.graph.degree(edge[1]) > self.network.graph.degree(edge[0]):
                    selected.add(edge[1])
                else:
                    selected.add(edge[0])
            return list(selected)

# 验证代码
def test_network_contagion():
    """测试网络信任传染"""
    print("=== 网络信任传染验证 ===")
    
    # 构建测试网络
    network = TrustContagionNetwork()
    
    # 添加节点
    founders = [
        FounderNode('A', 0.9, 0.8, 0.9, 'AI', 'Series A'),
        FounderNode('B', 0.9, 0.6, 0.7, 'AI', 'Seed'),
        FounderNode('C', 0.9, 0.9, 0.5, 'Bio', 'Series B'),
        FounderNode('D', 0.9, 0.7, 0.6, 'AI', 'Seed'),
        FounderNode('E', 0.9, 0.5, 0.4, 'Bio', 'Series A')
    ]
    
    for f in founders:
        network.add_founder(f)
    
    # 添加边（共同投资人、同行等）
    network.add_influence_edge('A', 'B', 0.8, 'investor_overlap')
    network.add_influence_edge('A', 'C', 0.4, 'sector_peers')
    network.add_influence_edge('B', 'D', 0.6, 'sector_peers')
    network.add_influence_edge('C', 'E', 0.5, 'investor_overlap')
    network.add_influence_edge('A', 'D', 0.3, 'media_attention')
    
    # 模拟级联
    trajectories = network.simulate_cascade('A', steps=20)
    print(f"级联模拟完成，节点数: {len(trajectories)}")
    print(f"节点A最终信任状态: {trajectories['A'][-1]:.2f}")
    
    # 系统性风险
    risk = network.calculate_systemic_risk()
    print(f"\n系统性风险分数: {risk['systemic_risk_score']:.2f}")
    print(f"最大连通分量占比: {risk['giant_component_ratio']:.2%}")
    
    # 超级传播者
    spreaders = network.identify_super_spreaders(3)
    print(f"\n超级传播者: {spreaders}")
    
    # 免疫策略
    immunization = ImmunizationStrategy(network)
    targets = immunization.calculate_optimal_immunization(2, 'targeted')
    print(f"目标免疫节点: {targets}")
    
    print("✓ 网络信任传染验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_network_contagion()

第十一轮：因果推断引擎——从预测到干预

11.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：之前的系统专注于预测（P(trust_collapse|features)），但创始人真正需要的是干预（P(trust_collapse|do(intervention))）。这要求使用因果推断（Causal Inference）和Do-Calculus。

数学重构： 使用结构因果模型（SCM）和潜在结果框架（Potential Outcomes）。

其中  是混淆变量（Confounders）。

11.2 代码实现：因果干预引擎

# causal_inference_engine.py
# 因果推断引擎：从预测到干预

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
import statsmodels.api as sm
from scipy import stats

@dataclass
class CausalGraph:
    """因果图：节点和边的有向图"""
    nodes: List[str]
    edges: List[Tuple[str, str]]  # (cause, effect)
    confounders: Dict[str, List[str]]  # 每个节点的混淆变量
    
    def get_parents(self, node: str) -> List[str]:
        """获取父节点（直接原因）"""
        return [edge[0] for edge in self.edges if edge[1] == node]
    
    def get_ancestors(self, node: str) -> Set[str]:
        """获取祖先节点（所有间接原因）"""
        ancestors = set()
        parents = self.get_parents(node)
        ancestors.update(parents)
        for parent in parents:
            ancestors.update(self.get_ancestors(parent))
        return ancestors

class DoCalculusEngine:
    """Do-Calculus引擎：计算干预效果"""
    
    def __init__(self, causal_graph: CausalGraph):
        self.graph = causal_graph
        self.data_cache = []
        
    def identify(self, 
                 treatment: str, 
                 outcome: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        识别公式：确定P(Y|do(X))是否可识别
        
        基于后门准则、前门准则等
        """
        # 检查后门准则：是否存在混淆变量集Z，阻断X到Y的所有后门路径
        backdoor_sets = self._find_backdoor_sets(treatment, outcome)
        
        if backdoor_sets:
            return f"backdoor_adjustment:Z={backdoor_sets[0]}"
        
        # 检查前门准则
        frontdoor_path = self._find_frontdoor_path(treatment, outcome)
        if frontdoor_path:
            return f"frontdoor_adjustment:M={frontdoor_path}"
        
        return None  # 不可识别
    
    def _find_backdoor_sets(self, 
                            treatment: str, 
                            outcome: str) -> List[List[str]]:
        """寻找满足后门准则的变量集"""
        # 简化实现：寻找所有非后代节点（潜在混淆变量）
        descendants = self._get_descendants(treatment)
        candidates = [n for n in self.graph.nodes 
                     if n not in descendants and n != treatment and n != outcome]
        
        # 验证是否阻断所有后门路径（简化：假设所有候选都满足）
        if candidates:
            return [candidates]
        return []
    
    def _get_descendants(self, node: str) -> Set[str]:
        """获取后代节点"""
        descendants = set()
        children = [edge[1] for edge in self.graph.edges if edge[0] == node]
        descendants.update(children)
        for child in children:
            descendants.update(self._get_descendants(child))
        return descendants
    
    def _find_frontdoor_path(self, 
                              treatment: str, 
                              outcome: str) -> Optional[List[str]]:
        """寻找前门路径（中介变量）"""
        # 寻找中介变量M：X -> M -> Y，且没有X到Y的直接路径
        direct_edge = (treatment, outcome) in self.graph.edges
        
        if not direct_edge:
            # 寻找中介
            mediators = []
            for edge1 in self.graph.edges:
                if edge1[0] == treatment:
                    for edge2 in self.graph.edges:
                        if edge2[0] == edge1[1] and edge2[1] == outcome:
                            mediators.append(edge1[1])
            
            if mediators:
                return mediators
        
        return None
    
    def estimate_ate(self, 
                     data: np.ndarray,
                     treatment: str,
                     outcome: str,
                     adjustment_set: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        估计平均处理效应（ATE）
        
        ATE = E[Y|do(X=1)] - E[Y|do(X=0)]
        """
        # 后门调整公式实现
        # 分层估计
        strata_effects = []
        
        # 这里简化实现，使用线性回归近似
        X = data[:, 0]  # 处理变量
        Y = data[:, 1]  # 结果变量
        Z = data[:, 2:] if data.shape[1] > 2 else np.zeros((len(X), 1))  # 混淆变量
        
        # 倾向得分估计（Propensity Score）
        ps_model = LogisticRegression()
        ps_model.fit(Z, X)
        propensity_scores = ps_model.predict_proba(Z)[:, 1]
        
        # IPW估计（Inverse Probability Weighting）
        weights_treated = X / (propensity_scores + 1e-6)
        weights_control = (1 - X) / (1 - propensity_scores + 1e-6)
        
        ate_ipw = (np.sum(weights_treated * Y) / np.sum(weights_treated) -
                   np.sum(weights_control * Y) / np.sum(weights_control))
        
        # 置信区间（Bootstrap）
        bootstrap_ates = []
        for _ in range(100):
            idx = np.random.choice(len(X), len(X), replace=True)
            ate_boot = self._quick_ate(X[idx], Y[idx], propensity_scores[idx])
            bootstrap_ates.append(ate_boot)
        
        ci_lower, ci_upper = np.percentile(bootstrap_ates, [2.5, 97.5])
        
        return {
            'ate': float(ate_ipw),
            'ci_95': (float(ci_lower), float(ci_upper)),
            'p_value': self._calculate_p_value(bootstrap_ates),
            'interpretation': 'positive' if ate_ipw > 0 else 'negative' if ate_ipw < 0 else 'neutral'
        }
    
    def _quick_ate(self, X, Y, ps):
        """快速ATE计算（用于Bootstrap）"""
        weights_t = X / (ps + 1e-6)
        weights_c = (1 - X) / (1 - ps + 1e-6)
        return (np.sum(weights_t * Y) / np.sum(weights_t) -
                np.sum(weights_c * Y) / np.sum(weights_c))
    
    def _calculate_p_value(self, bootstrap_distribution: List[float]) -> float:
        """计算p值（检验是否显著不为0）"""
        return 2 * min(
            np.mean(np.array(bootstrap_distribution) <= 0),
            np.mean(np.array(bootstrap_distribution) >= 0)
        )

class CounterfactualSimulator:
    """反事实模拟器：如果...会怎样"""
    
    def __init__(self, scm: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        scm: 结构因果模型
        例如: {'Y': '2*X + 3*Z + noise', 'X': 'Z + noise', ...}
        """
        self.scm = scm
        self.noise_distribution = stats.norm(0, 1)
        
    def simulate_counterfactual(self,
                                  factual_data: Dict[str, float],
                                  intervention: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        模拟反事实：在观察到 factual_data 的情况下，
        如果干预 intervention 发生，结果会怎样？
        
        三步法（Abduction, Action, Prediction）：
        1. Abduction: 从事实推断外生变量的值
        2. Action: 修改SCM以反映干预
        3. Prediction: 向前计算反事实结果
        """
        # 1. 推断外生变量（噪声项）
        noise = self._abduce_noise(factual_data)
        
        # 2. 修改SCM（Do操作）
        modified_scm = self._apply_intervention(intervention)
        
        # 3. 预测
        counterfactual = self._predict(modified_scm, noise, factual_data)
        
        return counterfactual
    
    def _abduce_noise(self, factual: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """推断外生噪声"""
        noise = {}
        for var, equation in self.scm.items():
            # 简化：假设线性关系，反解噪声
            if var in factual:
                # 这里应该解析方程并反解，简化实现
                noise[var] = factual[var] * 0.1  # 假设噪声占10%
        return noise
    
    def _apply_intervention(self, intervention: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """应用干预：删除 incoming edges，设为常数"""
        modified = self.scm.copy()
        for var, value in intervention.items():
            modified[var] = lambda noise, context=value: value  # 常量函数
        return modified
    
    def _predict(self, 
                 modified_scm: Dict, 
                 noise: Dict,
                 context: Dict) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """向前预测"""
        result = context.copy()
        for var, equation in modified_scm.items():
            if callable(equation):
                result[var] = equation(noise.get(var, 0))
            else:
                # 解析并计算公式（简化）
                result[var] = equation  # 假设已经是值
        return result

class InterventionRecommender:
    """干预推荐器：基于因果效应的最优干预"""
    
    def __init__(self, causal_engine: DoCalculusEngine):
        self.engine = causal_engine
        
    def recommend_interventions(self,
                                   current_state: Dict[str, float],
                                   target_outcome: str,
                                   outcome_target: float,
                                   available_interventions: List[str]) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        推荐干预措施：最大化目标结果
        
        返回排序的干预列表，包含预期效果
        """
        recommendations = []
        
        for intervention in available_interventions:
            # 估计不同强度干预的效果
            for intensity in [0.5, 1.0, 1.5]:
                effect = self._estimate_intervention_effect(
                    intervention, intensity, target_outcome
                )
                
                recommendations.append({
                    'intervention': intervention,
                    'intensity': intensity,
                    'expected_outcome_change': effect,
                    'cost_benefit_ratio': self._calculate_cost_benefit(intervention, effect),
                    'confidence': self._estimate_confidence(intervention)
                })
        
        # 按成本效益排序
        recommendations.sort(key=lambda x: x['cost_benefit_ratio'], reverse=True)
        return recommendations[:5]
    
    def _estimate_intervention_effect(self, 
                                      intervention: str, 
                                      intensity: float,
                                      outcome: str) -> float:
        """估计干预效果（简化）"""
        # 使用预计算的因果效应或在线估计
        return np.random.normal(0.3, 0.1) * intensity  # 模拟
    
    def _calculate_cost_benefit(self, 
                                 intervention: str, 
                                 effect: float) -> float:
        """计算成本效益比"""
        costs = {
            'weekly_sync': 1.0,
            'third_party_mediation': 5.0,
            'equity_restructuring': 10.0,
            'founder_coaching': 3.0
        }
        cost = costs.get(intervention, 5.0)
        return effect / cost
    
    def _estimate_confidence(self, intervention: str) -> float:
        """估计置信度"""
        return np.random.uniform(0.7, 0.95)

# 验证代码
def test_causal_inference():
    """测试因果推断引擎"""
    print("=== 因果推断验证 ===")
    
    # 1. 构建因果图
    # 假设：沟通频率 -> 信任水平 -> 合伙人稳定性
    #       外部压力 -> 沟通频率
    #       外部压力 -> 合伙人稳定性（混淆变量）
    graph = CausalGraph(
        nodes=['external_pressure', 'communication_freq', 'trust_level', 'stability'],
        edges=[
            ('external_pressure', 'communication_freq'),
            ('external_pressure', 'stability'),
            ('communication_freq', 'trust_level'),
            ('trust_level', 'stability')
        ],
        confounders={'stability': ['external_pressure']}
    )
    
    engine = DoCalculusEngine(graph)
    
    # 2. 检验可识别性
    identification = engine.identify('communication_freq', 'stability')
    print(f"可识别性: {identification}")
    assert identification is not None
    
    # 3. 模拟数据估计ATE
    np.random.seed(42)
    n = 1000
    Z = np.random.randn(n)  # 外部压力
    X = (Z + np.random.randn(n) > 0).astype(int)  # 沟通频率（受压力影响）
    Y = 2*X + 3*Z + np.random.randn(n)  # 稳定性
    
    data = np.column_stack([X, Y, Z])
    
    ate_result = engine.estimate_ate(data, 'communication_freq', 'stability', ['external_pressure'])
    print(f"\n干预效果(ATE): {ate_result['ate']:.3f}")
    print(f"95%置信区间: {ate_result['ci_95']}")
    print(f"P值: {ate_result['p_value']:.3f}")
    
    # 4. 反事实模拟
    scm = {
        'stability': '2*communication + 3*pressure',
        'communication': 'pressure'
    }
    simulator = CounterfactualSimulator(scm)
    
    factual = {'communication': 0.5, 'pressure': 1.0, 'stability': 4.5}
    counterfactual = simulator.simulate_counterfactual(
        factual, 
        {'communication': 1.0}  # 如果沟通频率提高到1.0
    )
    print(f"\n反事实预测: {counterfactual}")
    
    # 5. 干预推荐
    recommender = InterventionRecommender(engine)
    recs = recommender.recommend_interventions(
        factual, 'stability', 6.0,
        ['weekly_sync', 'third_party_mediation', 'founder_coaching']
    )
    print(f"\n推荐干预（前3）:")
    for i, rec in enumerate(recs[:3], 1):
        print(f"  {i}. {rec['intervention']}: 预期改善{rec['expected_outcome_change']:.2f}, "
              f"性价比{rec['cost_benefit_ratio']:.2f}")
    
    print("✓ 因果推断验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_causal_inference()

第十二轮：神经符号架构（概要）

# neuro_symbolic_architecture.py
# 神经符号架构：感知+推理的可解释融合

class NeuralSymbolicTrustReasoner:
    """神经符号信任推理器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        # 神经部分：感知（BERT/GPT处理文本）
        self.neural_perceptor = transformers.AutoModel.from_pretrained('bert-base-chinese')
        # 符号部分：逻辑规则（一阶逻辑）
        self.symbolic_reasoner = PrologLogicEngine()
        
    def perceive_and_reason(self, text_log: str) -> Dict:
        """感知+推理"""
        # 1. 神经编码：文本->向量
        embeddings = self.neural_perceptor.encode(text_log)
        
        # 2. 符号化：向量->谓词
        predicates = self._vector_to_predicates(embeddings)
        # 例如：suspicion(high), communication_delayed(true)
        
        # 3. 符号推理：Prolog逻辑推导
        logical_conclusions = self.symbolic_reasoner.query(
            f"trust_collapse(X) :- {predicates}"
        )
        
        # 4. 神经-符号一致性检查
        consistency = self._check_consistency(embeddings, logical_conclusions)
        
        return {
            'neural_confidence': float(embeddings.confidence),
            'symbolic_proof': logical_conclusions,
            'integrated_decision': self._neuro_symbolic_integration(
                embeddings, logical_conclusions, consistency
            ),
            'explanation_chain': self._generate_explanation(predicates, logical_conclusions)
        }
    
    def _generate_explanation(self, predicates, conclusions) -> str:
        """生成可解释的逻辑链"""
        return f"检测到{predicates}，根据规则{conclusions}，推断出..."

第十三轮：多智能体强化学习（概要）

# multi_agent_rl.py
# 多智能体强化学习：合伙人匹配的涌现行为

class PartnerMatchingMARL:
    """合伙人匹配的多智能体RL"""
    
    def __init__(self, n_agents: int = 3):
        self.agents = [FounderAgent(i) for i in range(n_agents)]
        self.environment = PartnershipEnvironment()
        
        # 使用MADDPG（多智能体DDPG）或QMIX
        self.policy = MADDPGPolicy(n_agents, state_dim=20, action_dim=5)
        
    def simulate_partnership_formation(self, 
                                       n_episodes: int = 1000) -> Dict:
        """模拟合伙人形成过程"""
        outcomes = []
        
        for episode in range(n_episodes):
            states = self.environment.reset()
            episode_reward = 0
            
            while not self.environment.is_terminal():
                # 每个智能体选择动作（信任、资源投入、沟通等）
                actions = [agent.select_action(states[i]) 
                          for i, agent in enumerate(self.agents)]
                
                # 环境演化（联合动作导致状态转移）
                next_states, rewards, dones = self.environment.step(actions)
                
                # 存储经验
                self.policy.store_experience(states, actions, rewards, next_states, dones)
                
                states = next_states
                episode_reward += sum(rewards)
            
            # 训练
            self.policy.train()
            outcomes.append(self.environment.get_partnership_quality())
        
        return {
            'emergent_strategies': self._analyze_emergent_behaviors(),
            'equilibrium_type': self._classify_equilibrium(),
            'optimal_policy': self.policy.export_policy()
        }

第十四轮：对抗鲁棒性（概要）

# adversarial_robustness.py
# 对抗鲁棒性：AI安全与防御

class AdversarialDefenseSystem:
    """对抗防御系统"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.detector = AnomalyDetector()  # 检测异常输入
        self.sanitizer = InputSanitizer()  # 输入清洗
        self.certifier = RobustnessCertifier()  # 鲁棒性认证
        
    def defend(self, input_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """多层防御"""
        # 1. 异常检测（是否是 adversarial example?）
        anomaly_score = self.detector.score(input_data)
        if anomaly_score > 0.9:
            raise AdversarialInputDetected("可能的对抗样本")
        
        # 2. 输入清洗（去除扰动）
        clean_data = self.sanitizer.denoise(input_data, method='feature_squeezing')
        
        # 3. 随机化防御（输入转换）
        randomized_inputs = self._randomized_smoothing(clean_data, n_samples=100)
        
        # 4. 多数投票（平滑预测）
        predictions = [self.model.predict(inp) for inp in randomized_inputs]
        consensus = self._majority_vote(predictions)
        
        # 5. 鲁棒性认证（给出预测半径）
        certified_radius = self.certifier.calculate_radius(clean_data, consensus)
        
        return {
            'prediction': consensus,
            'certified_robustness_radius': certified_radius,
            'is_certified': certified_radius > 0.1,
            'anomaly_score': anomaly_score
        }

第十五轮：具身认知（概要）

# embodied_cognition.py
# 具身认知：生物信号融合

class EmbodiedTrustSensor:
    """具身信任传感器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.biosignal_processor = BioSignalProcessor()
        
    def ingest_multimodal_embodied(self,
                                   text: str,
                                   voice: np.ndarray,
                                   wearable_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """融合生物信号"""
        # 1. 生理信号解码
        hrv = wearable_data.get('heart_rate_variability')  # 心率变异性（压力指标）
        gsr = wearable_data.get('galvanic_skin_response')  # 皮电反应（焦虑）
        sleep_quality = wearable_data.get('sleep_score')    # 睡眠质量（认知功能）
        
        # 2. 具身特征提取
        physiological_stress = self._calculate_stress_index(hrv, gsr)
        cognitive_capacity = self._estimate_cognitive_capacity(sleep_quality)
        
        # 3. 具身信任推断（身体状态影响决策质量）
        embodied_trust_bias = self._calculate_embodied_bias(
            physiological_stress, cognitive_capacity
        )
        
        return {
            'raw_biosignals': {'hrv': hrv, 'gsr': gsr, 'sleep': sleep_quality},
            'physiological_stress_index': physiological_stress,
            'cognitive_capacity_estimate': cognitive_capacity,
            'embodied_decision_bias': embodied_trust_bias,
            'calibrated_prediction': self._apply_embodied_calibration(
                text_analysis, embodied_trust_bias
            )
        }
    
    def _calculate_stress_index(self, hrv: float, gsr: float) -> float:
        """计算生理压力指数"""
        # HRV越低、GSR越高=压力越大
        return (1 - hrv/100) * 0.5 + (gsr/10) * 0.5

第十六轮：动态知识图谱（概要）

# dynamic_knowledge_graph.py
# 动态知识图谱：持续学习系统

class DynamicTrustKnowledgeGraph:
    """动态信任知识图谱"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.graph = TemporalKnowledgeGraph()  # 时序图数据库
        self.learner = ContinualLearningModule()  # 持续学习模块
        
    def ingest_case(self, decision_case: Dict):
        """摄入案例：终身学习"""
        # 1. 编码为图结构（节点：实体，边：关系，时间戳：时序）
        triples = self._extract_triples(decision_case)
        timestamp = decision_case['timestamp']
        
        # 2. 动态图更新（处理概念漂移）
        self.graph.update(triples, timestamp)
        
        # 3. 检测分布变化（是否需要更新模型？）
        if self._detect_concept_drift(triples):
            # 弹性权重整合（EWC）防止灾难性遗忘
            self.learner.update_with_ewc(self.graph, new_data=triples)
        
        # 4. 图推理：链接预测（发现新的隐含关系）
        new_relations = self.graph.predict_links(
            entity_type='founder',
            relation_type='likely_trust_collapse_with'
        )
        
        return {
            'graph_stats': self.graph.get_statistics(),
            'newly_discovered_patterns': new_relations,
            'model_update_status': 'updated' if self._detect_concept_drift(triples) else 'stable'
        }
    
    def _detect_concept_drift(self, new_triples: List) -> bool:
        """检测概念漂移：新数据是否与历史分布不同"""
        # 使用KL散度或Wasserstein距离
        historical_dist = self.graph.get_embedding_distribution(last_n=1000)
        new_dist = self._embed_triples(new_triples)
        
        drift_score = self._calculate_wasserstein(historical_dist, new_dist)
        return drift_score > 0.5  # 阈值
