第二十二轮：代数拓扑与持续同调——拓扑数据分析

22.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：信任关系的”盲区”不是统计学意义上的数据缺失，而是拓扑结构中的洞（Holes）——某些决策配置在数学上不可能被当前认知框架捕捉。

数学重构： 使用持续同调（Persistent Homology）和Mapper算法： - 0维同调 ：连通分量（不同的信任社群） - 1维同调 ：循环结构（循环依赖/相互猜疑） - 2维同调 ：空洞（三维盲区，如”家族-情感-股权”的三重纠缠）

22.2 代码实现：拓扑缺陷检测器

# topological_data_analysis.py
# 拓扑数据分析：信任空间的形状识别

import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
from scipy.spatial.distance import pdist, squareform
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass
class Simplex:
    """单纯形：拓扑构建块"""
    vertices: Tuple[int, ...]  # 顶点索引
    dimension: int
    birth_time: float  # 出现时的时间/尺度参数
    death_time: float = float('inf')  # 消失时间（持续同调）

class VietorisRipsComplex:
    """Vietoris-Rips复形：从点云构建拓扑"""
    
    def __init__(self, max_dimension: int = 3):
        self.max_dim = max_dimension
        self.simplices: Dict[int, List[Simplex]] = defaultdict(list)
        
    def build_from_point_cloud(self, 
                               points: np.ndarray,
                               max_radius: float) -> None:
        """
        从点云构建VR复形
        
        points: 每个点是信任状态的特征向量 [entropy, communication, alignment, ...]
        """
        n_points = len(points)
        distances = squareform(pdist(points))
        
        # 0-单纯形（顶点）：总是存在
        for i in range(n_points):
            self.simplices[0].append(Simplex((i,), 0, 0.0))
        
        # 1-单纯形（边）：距离 < 2*radius 的连边
        edges = []
        for i in range(n_points):
            for j in range(i+1, n_points):
                if distances[i, j] < 2 * max_radius:
                    edge = Simplex((i, j), 1, distances[i, j])
                    self.simplices[1].append(edge)
                    edges.append((i, j))
        
        # 高维单纯形：团（clique）检测
        for dim in range(2, self.max_dim + 1):
            self._build_higher_simplices(edges, dim, points, distances, max_radius)
    
    def _build_higher_simplices(self, 
                                 edges: List[Tuple[int, int]], 
                                 dimension: int,
                                 points: np.ndarray,
                                 distances: np.ndarray,
                                 max_radius: float):
        """构建高维单纯形（寻找团）"""
        from itertools import combinations
        
        # 找到所有可能的dim维单纯形
        n_points = len(points)
        for combo in combinations(range(n_points), dimension + 1):
            # 检查是否构成团（所有边都存在且距离<阈值）
            is_clique = True
            max_dist = 0.0
            
            for i, j in combinations(combo, 2):
                if distances[i, j] >= 2 * max_radius:
                    is_clique = False
                    break
                max_dist = max(max_dist, distances[i, j])
            
            if is_clique:
                simplex = Simplex(combo, dimension, max_dist)
                self.simplices[dimension].append(simplex)

class PersistentHomologyCalculator:
    """持续同调计算器：识别拓扑特征的寿命"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.barcode: Dict[int, List[Tuple[float, float]]] = defaultdict(list)
        self.betti_numbers_history: List[Dict[int, int]] = []
        
    def calculate_persistence(self, 
                              filtration: List[Simplex]) -> Dict[int, List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
        """
        计算持续同调：追踪拓扑特征的出生和死亡
        
        返回条形码（barcode）：每个特征[出生, 死亡]
        """
        # 使用矩阵约简算法（标准持续同调算法）
        # 简化实现：基于并查集的0维，基于边界矩阵的高维
        
        # 按出生时间排序（filtration顺序）
        sorted_simplices = sorted(filtration, key=lambda s: s.birth_time)
        
        # 0维：连通分量（并查集）
        self._calculate_h0(sorted_simplices)
        
        # 1维及以上：边界矩阵约简（简化）
        for dim in range(1, 4):
            self._calculate_hn(sorted_simplices, dim)
        
        return dict(self.barcode)
    
    def _calculate_h0(self, simplices: List[Simplex]):
        """计算0维持续同调（连通分量）"""
        parent = {}
        
        def find(x):
            if x not in parent:
                parent[x] = x
                return x
            if parent[x] != x:
                parent[x] = find(parent[x])
            return parent[x]
        
        def union(x, y):
            px, py = find(x), find(y)
            if px != py:
                parent[px] = py
        
        current_components = set()
        
        for simplex in simplices:
            if simplex.dimension == 0:
                vertex = simplex.vertices[0]
                current_components.add(vertex)
                # 记录出生
                self.barcode[0].append((simplex.birth_time, float('inf')))
                
            elif simplex.dimension == 1:
                # 边连接两个顶点，可能合并分量
                v1, v2 = simplex.vertices
                if find(v1) != find(v2):
                    union(v1, v2)
                    # 一个分量死亡（合并）
                    # 简化：记录死亡时间
                    for i, (birth, death) in enumerate(self.barcode[0]):
                        if death == float('inf') and find(v1) == find(v2):
                            self.barcode[0][i] = (birth, simplex.birth_time)
                            break
    
    def _calculate_hn(self, simplices: List[Simplex], n: int):
        """计算n维持续同调（简化实现）"""
        n_simplices = [s for s in simplices if s.dimension == n]
        
        for simplex in n_simplices:
            # 检查是否被更高维填充（死亡）
            # 简化：随机分配寿命
            lifespan = np.random.exponential(0.5)
            self.barcode[n].append((simplex.birth_time, simplex.birth_time + lifespan))

class TopologyBlindSpotDetector:
    """拓扑盲区检测器：识别决策空间的洞"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.vr_complex = VietorisRipsComplex(max_dimension=3)
        self.homology_calc = PersistentHomologyCalculator()
        
    def analyze_trust_decision_space(self,
                                     historical_decisions: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """
        分析信任决策空间的拓扑结构
        
        返回发现的拓扑缺陷（洞）
        """
        # 1. 提取特征向量（每个决策是一个点）
        points = self._extract_decision_vectors(historical_decisions)
        
        # 2. 构建VR复形（多尺度）
        self.vr_complex.build_from_point_cloud(points, max_radius=1.0)
        
        # 3. 收集所有单纯形
        all_simplices = []
        for dim, simplices in self.vr_complex.simplices.items():
            all_simplices.extend(simplices)
        
        # 4. 计算持续同调
        barcode = self.homology_calc.calculate_persistence(all_simplices)
        
        # 5. 识别显著特征（长寿命的拓扑特征）
        significant_features = self._identify_significant_features(barcode)
        
        # 6. 定位盲区（2维洞以上）
        blind_spots = self._locate_blind_spots(significant_features, points)
        
        return {
            'betti_numbers': self._calculate_betti_numbers(barcode),
            'significant_loops': significant_features.get(1, []),  # 1维洞（循环）
            'voids': significant_features.get(2, []),  # 2维洞（盲区）
            'blind_spot_locations': blind_spots,
            'topological_complexity': len(significant_features)
        }
    
    def _extract_decision_vectors(self, decisions: List[Dict]) -> np.ndarray:
        """提取决策特征向量"""
        vectors = []
        for d in decisions:
            vec = [
                d.get('cognitive_load', 0.5),
                d.get('emotional_intensity', 0.5),
                d.get('stake_size', 0.5),
                d.get('time_pressure', 0.5),
                d.get('information_completeness', 0.5)
            ]
            vectors.append(vec)
        return np.array(vectors)
    
    def _identify_significant_features(self, 
                                        barcode: Dict[int, List[Tuple[float, float]]],
                                        min_persistence: float = 0.3) -> Dict[int, List]:
        """识别显著特征（持续时间长）"""
        significant = {}
        for dim, intervals in barcode.items():
            sig = [(birth, death) for birth, death in intervals 
                   if death - birth > min_persistence]
            if sig:
                significant[dim] = sig
        return significant
    
    def _locate_blind_spots(self, 
                            features: Dict[int, List], 
                            points: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:
        """定位盲区中心（洞的质心）"""
        blind_spots = []
        
        if 2 in features:  # 2维洞
            for birth, death in features[2]:
                # 找到对应尺度的点
                radius = (birth + death) / 2
                # 计算该尺度下的覆盖盲区
                spot = {
                    'dimension': 2,
                    'persistence': death - birth,
                    'description': '三维盲区：涉及股权、情感、权力的三重纠缠',
                    'centroid': np.mean(points, axis=0).tolist(),
                    'radius': radius,
                    'risk_level': 'CRITICAL' if death - birth > 1.0 else 'HIGH'
                }
                blind_spots.append(spot)
        
        return blind_spots
    
    def _calculate_betti_numbers(self, barcode: Dict) -> Dict[int, int]:
        """计算Betti数（各维洞的数量）"""
        betti = {}
        for dim, intervals in barcode.items():
            # 未死亡的特征（无穷大）是活跃洞
            alive = sum(1 for birth, death in intervals if death == float('inf'))
            betti[dim] = alive
        return betti

class MapperAlgorithm:
    """Mapper算法：拓扑简化与可视化"""
    
    def __init__(self, filter_function: Callable = None):
        self.filter = filter_function or self._default_filter
        
    def _default_filter(self, x: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """默认滤波函数：主成分投影"""
        return np.sum(x)  # 简化
        
    def build_mapper_graph(self,
                           points: np.ndarray,
                           resolution: int = 10,
                           overlap: float = 0.5) -> Dict:
        """
        构建Mapper图：拓扑网络简化
        
        步骤：
        1. 用滤波函数划分水平集
        2. 在每个水平集内聚类
        3. 连接重叠的聚类
        """
        # 1. 计算滤波值
        filter_values = np.array([self.filter(p) for p in points])
        
        # 2. 划分区间（带有重叠）
        min_val, max_val = filter_values.min(), filter_values.max()
        interval_width = (max_val - min_val) / resolution
        
        clusters = []
        edges = []
        
        for i in range(resolution):
            # 区间边界（带重叠）
            left = min_val + i * interval_width - overlap * interval_width
            right = min_val + (i + 1) * interval_width + overlap * interval_width
            
            # 选择该区间内的点
            mask = (filter_values >= left) & (filter_values <= right)
            cluster_points = points[mask]
            
            if len(cluster_points) > 0:
                # 聚类（简化：单链接）
                cluster_id = len(clusters)
                clusters.append({
                    'id': cluster_id,
                    'points': cluster_points,
                    'interval': (left, right),
                    'size': len(cluster_points)
                })
                
                # 检查与前一cluster的连接
                if clusters and i > 0:
                    prev_cluster = clusters[-2]
                    # 如果有重叠的点，添加边
                    if self._have_common_points(prev_cluster['points'], cluster_points):
                        edges.append((prev_cluster['id'], cluster_id))
        
        return {
            'nodes': len(clusters),
            'edges': edges,
            'clusters': clusters,
            'topological_summary': self._summarize_shape(clusters, edges)
        }
    
    def _have_common_points(self, set1: np.ndarray, set2: np.ndarray) -> bool:
        """检查两个集合是否有共同元素（简化）"""
        return len(set1) > 0 and len(set2) > 0
    
    def _summarize_shape(self, clusters: List[Dict], edges: List[Tuple]) -> str:
        """总结拓扑形状"""
        n_clusters = len(clusters)
        n_edges = len(edges)
        
        if n_edges < n_clusters - 1:
            return "不连通（多个信任孤岛）"
        elif n_edges == n_clusters - 1:
            return "树状结构（层级信任）"
        else:
            return "网络结构（复杂信任网，含循环）"

# 验证代码
def test_topological_analysis():
    """测试拓扑分析"""
    print("=== 代数拓扑验证 ===")
    
    # 创建测试数据（含明显聚类和洞）
    np.random.seed(42)
    
    # 三个聚类（三个信任社群）
    cluster1 = np.random.randn(10, 3) + np.array([0, 0, 0])
    cluster2 = np.random.randn(10, 3) + np.array([5, 0, 0])
    cluster3 = np.random.randn(10, 3) + np.array([2.5, 5, 0])
    
    # 环形结构（循环猜疑）
    angles = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 20)
    ring = np.array([[3 + np.cos(a), 3 + np.sin(a), 0] for a in angles])
    
    points = np.vstack([cluster1, cluster2, cluster3, ring])
    
    # 构建VR复形
    vr = VietorisRipsComplex(max_dimension=2)
    vr.build_from_point_cloud(points, max_radius=1.5)
    
    print(f"0-单纯形数: {len(vr.simplices[0])}")
    print(f"1-单纯形数: {len(vr.simplices[1])}")
    print(f"2-单纯形数: {len(vr.simplices[2])}")
    
    # 计算持续同调
    hom_calc = PersistentHomologyCalculator()
    all_simplices = []
    for dim in vr.simplices:
        all_simplices.extend(vr.simplices[dim])
    
    barcode = hom_calc.calculate_persistence(all_simplices)
    
    for dim, intervals in barcode.items():
        if intervals:
            print(f"H_{dim} 条形码: {len(intervals)}个特征")
    
    # 盲区检测
    detector = TopologyBlindSpotDetector()
    mock_decisions = [
        {'cognitive_load': 0.9, 'emotional_intensity': 0.8, 'stake_size': 0.9,
         'time_pressure': 0.3, 'information_completeness': 0.2},
        {'cognitive_load': 0.2, 'emotional_intensity': 0.3, 'stake_size': 0.1,
         'time_pressure': 0.9, 'information_completeness': 0.9}
    ] * 20
    
    result = detector.analyze_trust_decision_space(mock_decisions)
    print(f"\n拓扑复杂度: {result['topological_complexity']}")
    print(f"Betti数: {result['betti_numbers']}")
    print(f"发现盲区: {len(result['blind_spots'])}")
    
    # Mapper算法
    mapper = MapperAlgorithm()
    graph = mapper.build_mapper_graph(points[:30], resolution=5)
    print(f"\nMapper图节点: {graph['nodes']}, 边: {len(graph['edges'])}")
    print(f"拓扑摘要: {graph['topological_summary']}")
    
    print("✓ 代数拓扑验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_topological_analysis()

第二十三轮：信息热力学与Landauer原理——计算的物理极限

23.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：信任分析不仅是计算问题，也是热力学过程。每次信息擦除（forgetting past observations）都有能量成本（Landauer极限 ）。

数学重构： - 信息-热力学第二定律： - 麦克斯韦妖与信任决策：决策者作为”妖”，通过信息获取降低熵，但需要消耗能量（认知负荷） - 可逆计算：设计零能耗的信任分析（尽可能使用可逆逻辑门）

23.2 代码实现：热力学优化引擎

# information_thermodynamics.py
# 信息热力学引擎：计算的能量效率优化

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib

@dataclass
class ThermodynamicState:
    """热力学状态：信息与能量的统一"""
    information_entropy: float  # 香农熵（比特）
    thermodynamic_entropy: float  # 热力学熵（焦耳/开尔文）
    temperature: float  # 认知"温度"（决策急迫性）
    energy_cost: float  # 累积能量消耗（焦耳）
    irreversibility: float  # 不可逆操作比例

class LandauerCalculator:
    """Landauer计算器：信息擦除的能量成本"""
    
    def __init__(self, temperature: float = 310.0):  # 人体温度310K
        self.k_B = 1.380649e-23  # 玻尔兹曼常数
        self.T = temperature
        self.landauer_limit = self.k_B * self.T * np.log(2)  # 每比特 ~3e-21 焦耳
        
    def calculate_erasure_cost(self, n_bits: int) -> float:
        """
        计算擦除n比特信息的能量成本
        
        E = n * k_B * T * ln(2)
        """
        return n_bits * self.landauer_limit
    
    def calculate_information_to_energy(self, 
                                         entropy_reduction: float) -> float:
        """
        信息熵减少对应的能量消耗
        
        当系统通过信息获取降低不确定性（熵），
        相当于"擦除"了环境的不确定性，需要能量
        """
        # 将香农熵（比特）转换为热力学熵
        thermodynamic_entropy_change = entropy_reduction * self.k_B * np.log(2)
        
        # 最小能量成本
        min_energy = thermodynamic_entropy_change * self.T
        
        return min_energy

class ReversibleTrustComputer:
    """可逆信任计算机：接近零能耗的计算"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.reversible_operations = 0
        self.irreversible_operations = 0
        self.landauer_calc = LandauerCalculator()
        
    def compute_trust_update_reversible(self,
                                         prior_belief: np.ndarray,
                                         new_evidence: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float]:
        """
        可逆的信任更新：使用Toffoli门（可控可控非门）
        
        可逆计算不擦除信息，理论能耗为零
        """
        # 模拟可逆的贝叶斯更新
        # 保留历史状态，不覆盖（计算历史学）
        
        # 创建新状态而不删除旧状态（可逆性）
        updated_belief = self._reversible_bayes_update(prior_belief, new_evidence)
        
        # 记录操作（不擦除中间结果）
        self.reversible_operations += 1
        
        # 实际能耗主要来自存储而非计算
        storage_cost = self._calculate_storage_cost(prior_belief) * 1e-30  # 接近零
        
        return updated_belief, storage_cost
    
    def compute_trust_update_irreversible(self,
                                            prior_belief: np.ndarray,
                                            new_evidence: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float]:
        """
        不可逆的信任更新：传统计算（擦除中间结果）
        """
        # 标准贝叶斯更新（擦除旧信念）
        updated_belief = self._standard_bayes_update(prior_belief, new_evidence)
        
        # 擦除旧信念的能量成本
        n_bits_erased = self._count_bits(prior_belief)
        energy_cost = self.landauer_calc.calculate_erasure_cost(n_bits_erased)
        
        self.irreversible_operations += 1
        
        return updated_belief, energy_cost
    
    def _reversible_bayes_update(self, prior, evidence):
        """可逆贝叶斯更新（保留计算历史）"""
        # 使用贝叶斯定理但不覆盖先验
        likelihood = np.dot(evidence, prior)
        posterior = (likelihood * prior) / (np.sum(likelihood * prior) + 1e-10)
        return np.vstack([prior, posterior])  # 保留历史
    
    def _standard_bayes_update(self, prior, evidence):
        """标准（不可逆）贝叶斯更新"""
        likelihood = np.dot(evidence, prior)
        posterior = (likelihood * prior) / (np.sum(likelihood * prior) + 1e-10)
        return posterior  # 先验丢失
    
    def _count_bits(self, array: np.ndarray) -> int:
        """计算数组的信息量（比特）"""
        # 简化：每个float64为64比特
        return array.size * 64

class MaxwellDemonTrustOptimizer:
    """麦克斯韦妖信任优化器：信息作为资源"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.information_reservoir = 0.0  # 信息储备（负熵）
        self.work_extracted = 0.0  # 提取的"功"（决策效用）
        
    def acquire_information(self, 
                           observation: Dict,
                           prior_uncertainty: float) -> float:
        """
        获取信息：通过观察降低不确定性
        
        类似于麦克斯韦妖通过测量分子速度获取信息
        """
        # 计算信息增益（互信息）
        posterior_uncertainty = self._calculate_posterior_uncertainty(observation)
        information_gain = prior_uncertainty - posterior_uncertainty
        
        self.information_reservoir += information_gain
        
        return information_gain
    
    def extract_work(self, 
                     decision: str,
                     information_cost: float) -> float:
        """
        提取"功"：利用信息做有用决策
        
        权衡：信息获取成本 vs 决策收益
        """
        if self.information_reservoir < information_cost:
            return 0.0  # 信息不足，无法做可靠决策
        
        # 消耗信息储备
        self.information_reservoir -= information_cost
        
        # 计算决策效用（提取的功）
        utility = self._calculate_decision_utility(decision)
        self.work_extracted += utility
        
        return utility
    
    def optimize_information_acquisition(self,
                                          possible_observations: List[Dict],
                                          budget: float) -> List[str]:
        """
        优化信息获取策略：在能量预算内最大化信息获取
        
        解决：哪些观察最值得做？
        """
        # 计算每个观察的预期信息增益和能量成本
        candidates = []
        for obs in possible_observations:
            info_gain = obs['expected_information_gain']
            cost = obs['energy_cost']  # 获取该信息的认知/时间成本
            
            efficiency = info_gain / cost if cost > 0 else float('inf')
            candidates.append((obs['id'], efficiency, cost, info_gain))
        
        # 背包问题：在预算内选择最高效的信息源
        selected = self._knapsack_select(candidates, budget)
        
        return selected
    
    def _knapsack_select(self, 
                         items: List[Tuple], 
                         budget: float) -> List[str]:
        """背包算法选择最优信息源"""
        # 简化：贪心算法（按效率排序）
        sorted_items = sorted(items, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        
        selected = []
        remaining_budget = budget
        
        for item_id, efficiency, cost, _ in sorted_items:
            if cost <= remaining_budget:
                selected.append(item_id)
                remaining_budget -= cost
        
        return selected

class ThermodynamicTrustEfficiency:
    """热力学信任效率分析器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.landauer = LandauerCalculator()
        
    def analyze_decision_process(self,
                                  process_history: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """
        分析决策过程的热力学效率
        
        评估：是否浪费了信息（能量）？
        """
        total_info_erased = 0
        total_energy_used = 0
        reversible_ratio = []
        
        for step in process_history:
            if step['operation_type'] == 'belief_update':
                if step['method'] == 'reversible':
                    cost = step.get('storage_cost', 0)
                    total_energy_used += cost
                    reversible_ratio.append(1)
                else:
                    bits_erased = step.get('bits_erased', 0)
                    cost = self.landauer.calculate_erasure_cost(bits_erased)
                    total_info_erased += bits_erased
                    total_energy_used += cost
                    reversible_ratio.append(0)
        
        efficiency = np.mean(reversible_ratio) if reversible_ratio else 0
        
        return {
            'total_information_erased_bits': total_info_erased,
            'total_energy_cost_joules': total_energy_used,
            'reversible_computation_ratio': efficiency,
            'thermodynamic_efficiency': 'OPTIMAL' if efficiency > 0.9 else 'STANDARD',
            'landauer_violation_risk': total_energy_used < self.landauer.landauer_limit * total_info_erased
        }

# 验证代码
def test_thermodynamics():
    """测试信息热力学"""
    print("=== 信息热力学验证 ===")
    
    # Landauer极限
    landauer = LandauerCalculator(temperature=310)
    cost_per_bit = landauer.landauer_limit
    print(f"Landauer极限 (310K): {cost_per_bit:.3e} 焦耳/比特")
    
    # 擦除1MB数据的能量成本
    mb_cost = landauer.calculate_erasure_cost(8 * 1024 * 1024)
    print(f"擦除1MB所需能量: {mb_cost:.3e} 焦耳")
    
    # 可逆vs不可逆计算
    computer = ReversibleTrustComputer()
    
    prior = np.array([0.5, 0.5])
    evidence = np.array([[0.8, 0.2], [0.3, 0.7]])
    
    # 可逆更新
    _, rev_cost = computer.compute_trust_update_reversible(prior, evidence)
    print(f"\n可逆计算能耗: {rev_cost:.3e} 焦耳")
    
    # 不可逆更新
    _, irrev_cost = computer.compute_trust_update_irreversible(prior, evidence)
    print(f"不可逆计算能耗: {irrev_cost:.3e} 焦耳")
    print(f"能效比: {irrev_cost / (rev_cost + 1e-30):.1f}x")
    
    # 麦克斯韦妖优化
    demon = MaxwellDemonTrustOptimizer()
    
    # 获取信息
    info_gain = demon.acquire_information(
        {'signal': 'strong_cooperation'},
        prior_uncertainty=1.0
    )
    print(f"\n信息获取: {info_gain:.2f} 比特")
    
    # 做决策
    work = demon.extract_work('increase_trust', information_cost=0.3)
    print(f"提取决策功: {work:.2f} 效用单位")
    print(f"剩余信息储备: {demon.information_reservoir:.2f} 比特")
    
    # 效率分析
    analyzer = ThermodynamicTrustEfficiency()
    history = [
        {'operation_type': 'belief_update', 'method': 'irreversible', 'bits_erased': 64},
        {'operation_type': 'belief_update', 'method': 'reversible', 'storage_cost': 1e-30},
        {'operation_type': 'belief_update', 'method': 'irreversible', 'bits_erased': 64}
    ]
    
    analysis = analyzer.analyze_decision_process(history)
    print(f"\n热力学效率分析:")
    print(f"  总擦除比特: {analysis['total_information_erased_bits']}")
    print(f"  可逆计算比例: {analysis['reversible_computation_ratio']:.1%}")
    
    print("✓ 信息热力学验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_thermodynamics()

第二十四轮：心理动力学与依恋理论——深层心理学

24.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：合伙人冲突的表层是利益分歧，深层是依恋模式（Attachment Styles）的碰撞： - 焦虑型（Anxious）：过度寻求确认，恐惧被抛弃 - 回避型（Avoidant）：回避亲密，强调独立 - 安全型（Secure）：健康依赖，有效沟通 - 紊乱型（Disorganized）：矛盾行为，信任崩塌高风险

24.2 代码实现：依恋动力学模型

# attachment_dynamics.py
# 依恋动力学：深层心理学建模

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum

class AttachmentStyle(Enum):
    """依恋类型"""
    SECURE = "secure"           # 安全型
    ANXIOUS = "anxious"         # 焦虑型（占有/监控）
    AVOIDANT = "avoidant"       # 回避型（疏离/冷暴力）
    DISORGANIZED = "disorganized"  # 紊乱型（矛盾/攻击）

@dataclass
class InternalWorkingModel:
    """内部工作模型：依恋的心理表征"""
    self_model: float  # 自我模型（-1=无价值，1=有价值）
    other_model: float  # 他人模型（-1=不可信，1=可信）
    anxiety_level: float  # 焦虑维度
    avoidance_level: float  # 回避维度

class AttachmentDynamicsEngine:
    """依恋动力学引擎"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        # Bartholomew-Horowitz模型的维度定义
        self.dimensions = {
            'anxiety': (-1, 1),    # 对被抛弃的恐惧
            'avoidance': (-1, 1)   # 对亲密的回避
        }
    
    def classify_attachment(self, 
                           behavioral_markers: Dict) -> AttachmentStyle:
        """
        基于行为标记分类依恋类型
        
        使用两维度模型：
        - 高焦虑 + 低回避 = 焦虑型
        - 低焦虑 + 高回避 = 回避型
        - 高焦虑 + 高回避 = 紊乱型
        - 低焦虑 + 低回避 = 安全型
        """
        anxiety = behavioral_markers.get('separation_distress', 0.0) * 2 - 1
        avoidance = behavioral_markers.get('intimacy_avoidance', 0.0) * 2 - 1
        
        # 分类逻辑
        if anxiety > 0.3 and avoidance > 0.3:
            return AttachmentStyle.DISORGANIZED
        elif anxiety > 0.3 and avoidance < -0.3:
            return AttachmentStyle.ANXIOUS
        elif anxiety < -0.3 and avoidance > 0.3:
            return AttachmentStyle.AVOIDANT
        else:
            return AttachmentStyle.SECURE
    
    def simulate_dyadic_interaction(self,
                                    founder_a: InternalWorkingModel,
                                    founder_b: InternalWorkingModel,
                                    n_steps: int = 100) -> Dict:
        """
        模拟双人依恋互动动力学
        
        基于分形吸引力模型（dynamical systems）
        """
        # 状态变量：亲密度、焦虑水平
        intimacy_a = np.zeros(n_steps)
        intimacy_b = np.zeros(n_steps)
        
        # 初始条件
        intimacy_a[0] = 0.5
        intimacy_b[0] = 0.5
        
        # 微分方程模拟（耦合系统）
        for t in range(1, n_steps):
            # 创始人A的动态
            di_a = self._attachment_dynamics(
                intimacy_a[t-1], intimacy_b[t-1],
                founder_a.anxiety_level, founder_a.avoidance_level
            )
            
            # 创始人B的动态
            di_b = self._attachment_dynamics(
                intimacy_b[t-1], intimacy_a[t-1],
                founder_b.anxiety_level, founder_b.avoidance_level
            )
            
            intimacy_a[t] = np.clip(intimacy_a[t-1] + di_a * 0.1, 0, 1)
            intimacy_b[t] = np.clip(intimacy_b[t-1] + di_b * 0.1, 0, 1)
        
        # 分析吸引子（长期模式）
        attractor_type = self._identify_attractor(intimacy_a, intimacy_b)
        
        return {
            'intimacy_trajectory_a': intimacy_a.tolist(),
            'intimacy_trajectory_b': intimacy_b.tolist(),
            'attractor_type': attractor_type,
            'relationship_stability': self._calculate_stability(intimacy_a, intimacy_b),
            'collision_risk': self._assess_collision(founder_a, founder_b)
        }
    
    def _attachment_dynamics(self, 
                            self_intimacy: float,
                            partner_intimacy: float,
                            anxiety: float,
                            avoidance: float) -> float:
        """依恋微分方程：亲密度变化率"""
        # 基础吸引力（想要亲密）
        approach = (1 - avoidance) * (1 - self_intimacy)
        
        # 分离焦虑（害怕失去）
        fear = anxiety * (partner_intimacy - self_intimacy)
        
        # 自我保护（回避型在压力大时撤退）
        withdrawal = avoidance * max(0, partner_intimacy - 0.7)
        
        return approach + fear - withdrawal
    
    def _identify_attractor(self, traj_a: np.ndarray, traj_b: np.ndarray) -> str:
        """识别吸引子类型"""
        # 分析最终状态
        final_diff = abs(traj_a[-10:].mean() - traj_b[-10:].mean())
        
        if final_diff < 0.1:
            return "同步吸引子（安全型配对）"
        elif final_diff > 0.4:
            return "追逐-逃避吸引子（焦虑-回避配对）"
        elif np.std(traj_a[-20:]) > 0.2:
            return "混沌吸引子（紊乱型，不可预测）"
        else:
            return "固定点吸引子（稳定但疏离）"
    
    def _calculate_stability(self, traj_a: np.ndarray, traj_b: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """计算关系稳定性（Lyapunov指数近似）"""
        # 简化：计算方差
        variance = np.var(traj_a) + np.var(traj_b)
        return max(0, 1 - variance)
    
    def _assess_collision(self, 
                         model_a: InternalWorkingModel,
                         model_b: InternalWorkingModel) -> str:
        """评估依恋碰撞风险"""
        # 焦虑型 + 回避型 = 地狱组合（追逐-逃避）
        if (model_a.anxiety_level > 0.5 and model_b.avoidance_level > 0.5) or \
           (model_b.anxiety_level > 0.5 and model_a.avoidance_level > 0.5):
            return "CRITICAL: 追逐-逃避死亡螺旋"
        
        # 紊乱型 + 任何 = 高风险
        if model_a.anxiety_level > 0.5 and model_a.avoidance_level > 0.5:
            return "HIGH: 一方紊乱型，不可预测冲突"
        
        return "MODERATE: 标准冲突模式"

class DefenseMechanismDetector:
    """防御机制检测器：心理动力学"""
    
    def detect_repression(self, text: str) -> float:
        """检测压抑（无法意识到冲突）"""
        # 关键词：回避具体话题，过度理性化
        indicators = ['没什么问题', '都是小事', '不想谈这个', '过去就算了']
        return sum(1 for ind in indicators if ind in text) / len(indicators)
    
    def detect_projection(self, 
                          founder_a_accusations: List[str],
                          founder_b_behaviors: List[str]) -> float:
        """检测投射（将自己的特质归因于他人）"""
        # 如果A指责B的特质实际上是A自己表现出的
        overlap = set(founder_a_accusations) & set(founder_b_behaviors)
        return len(overlap) / max(len(founder_a_accusations), 1)
    
    def detect_splitting(self, text: str) -> float:
        """检测分裂（全好或全坏的极端化）"""
        # 极端词汇使用
        extremes = ['完全', '绝对', '永远', '从不', '彻底', '毁灭']
        return sum(text.count(ext) for ext in extremes) / len(text)

class TransferenceAnalyzer:
    """移情分析器：将过去关系模式带入当前"""
    
    def analyze_transference(self,
                             founder_history: Dict,
                             current_partner_traits: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        分析移情：创始人是否将过去合伙人的形象投射到当前？
        """
        # 比较当前伴侣特质与过去重要他人
        similarity_to_father = self._calculate_similarity(
            current_partner_traits, founder_history.get('father_figure', {})
        )
        similarity_to_previous_partner = self._calculate_similarity(
            current_partner_traits, founder_history.get('previous_co_founder', {})
        )
        
        transference_strength = max(similarity_to_father, similarity_to_previous_partner)
        
        return {
            'transference_detected': transference_strength > 0.7,
            'transference_source': 'father' if similarity_to_father > similarity_to_previous_partner else 'previous_partner',
            'transference_strength': transference_strength,
            'likely_reaction': self._predict_transference_reaction(
                founder_history, transference_strength
            )
        }
    
    def _calculate_similarity(self, traits_a: Dict, traits_b: Dict) -> float:
        """计算特质相似度"""
        if not traits_a or not traits_b:
            return 0.0
        common_keys = set(traits_a.keys()) & set(traits_b.keys())
        if not common_keys:
            return 0.0
        return np.mean([1 - abs(traits_a[k] - traits_b[k]) for k in common_keys])
    
    def _predict_transference_reaction(self, 
                                       history: Dict,
                                       strength: float) -> str:
        """预测移情反应"""
        if 'previous_betrayal' in str(history):
            return "过度警惕背叛（基于过去创伤）"
        return "理想化倾向（基于早期安全关系）"

# 验证代码
def test_attachment_dynamics():
    """测试依恋动力学"""
    print("=== 依恋动力学验证 ===")
    
    engine = AttachmentDynamicsEngine()
    
    # 焦虑型创始人
    anxious = InternalWorkingModel(
        self_model=0.3, other_model=0.7,
        anxiety_level=0.8, avoidance_level=-0.2
    )
    
    # 回避型创始人
    avoidant = InternalWorkingModel(
        self_model=0.7, other_model=0.3,
        anxiety_level=-0.3, avoidance_level=0.8
    )
    
    # 模拟互动（地狱组合）
    result = engine.simulate_dyadic_interaction(anxious, avoidant, n_steps=50)
    
    print(f"吸引子类型: {result['attractor_type']}")
    print(f"碰撞风险: {result['collision_risk']}")
    print(f"稳定性评分: {result['relationship_stability']:.2f}")
    
    assert result['collision_risk'].startswith("CRITICAL")
    
    # 安全型组合
    secure = InternalWorkingModel(0.5, 0.5, -0.2, -0.2)
    secure_result = engine.simulate_dyadic_interaction(secure, secure)
    print(f"\n安全型组合稳定性: {secure_result['relationship_stability']:.2f}")
    assert secure_result['relationship_stability'] > anxious_result['relationship_stability']
    
    # 防御机制检测
    defense = DefenseMechanismDetector()
    repression_score = defense.detect_repression("没什么问题，都是小事，不想谈这个")
    print(f"\n压抑检测分数: {repression_score:.2f}")
    assert repression_score > 0.3
    
    print("✓ 依恋动力学验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_attachment_dynamics()

第二十五轮：自组织临界性与混沌边缘——相变理论

25.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：健康的关系运行在混沌边缘（Edge of Chaos）——既不是僵化的秩序（死寂），也不是彻底的混乱（崩溃），而是自组织临界性（Self-Organized Criticality, SOC）状态。

数学重构： - 沙堆模型（Bak-Tang-Wiesenfeld）：信任如沙粒，积累到一定程度引发崩塌（雪崩） - 幂律分布：崩塌规模遵循幂律（无特征尺度，不可预测具体事件但可预测分布） - 1/f噪声：健康关系的特征（粉红噪声），过度相关（白噪声）或过度无序（布朗噪声）都危险

25.2 代码实现：临界性监测器

# self_organized_criticality.py
# 自组织临界性：混沌边缘的信任管理

import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from collections import deque
import powerlaw  # 用于拟合幂律

@dataclass
class AvalancheEvent:
    """崩塌事件（雪崩）"""
    size: int  # 影响范围（多少人卷入）
    duration: int  # 持续时间（天）
    energy_released: float  # 情绪能量（冲突强度）
    trigger: str  # 触发因素

class SandpileTrustModel:
    """沙堆信任模型：SOC核心"""
    
    def __init__(self, grid_size: int = 20):
        self.size = grid_size
        self.grid = np.zeros((grid_size, grid_size))  # 信任张力积累
        self.threshold = 4.0  # 崩塌阈值（临界斜率）
        self.avalanche_history: List[AvalancheEvent] = []
        
    def add_trust_stress(self, 
                        location: Tuple[int, int],
                        amount: float):
        """添加信任应力（如未解决的冲突）"""
        x, y = location
        self.grid[x, y] += amount
        
        # 检查是否触发崩塌
        if self.grid[x, y] > self.threshold:
            return self._topple(x, y)
        return AvalancheEvent(0, 0, 0, 'none')
    
    def _topple(self, x: int, y: int) -> AvalancheEvent:
        """崩塌（沙粒重分配）"""
        size = 0
        duration = 0
        energy = 0.0
        
        # 使用栈实现DFS崩塌传播
        stack = [(x, y)]
        toppled = set()
        
        while stack:
            cx, cy = stack.pop()
            if (cx, cy) in toppled:
                continue
            
            if self.grid[cx, cy] > self.threshold:
                # 崩塌：向4邻居传递应力
                self.grid[cx, cy] -= 4.0
                toppled.add((cx, cy))
                size += 1
                energy += 4.0
                
                # 传递给邻居
                for dx, dy in [(0,1), (0,-1), (1,0), (-1,0)]:
                    nx, ny = cx + dx, cy + dy
                    if 0 <= nx < self.size and 0 <= ny < self.size:
                        self.grid[nx, ny] += 1.0
                        if self.grid[nx, ny] > self.threshold:
                            stack.append((nx, ny))
        
        event = AvalancheEvent(size, duration, energy, 'stress_overflow')
        self.avalanche_history.append(event)
        return event
    
    def analyze_criticality(self) -> Dict:
        """分析临界性状态"""
        if len(self.avalanche_history) < 10:
            return {'status': 'insufficient_data'}
        
        sizes = [a.size for a in self.avalanche_history if a.size > 0]
        
        # 拟合幂律分布
        fit = powerlaw.Fit(sizes, discrete=True)
        
        return {
            'power_law_alpha': fit.power_law.alpha,  # 幂指数
            'is_power_law': fit.power_law.xmin is not None,
            'critical_temperature': self._estimate_temperature(),
            'distance_to_critical': self._distance_to_critical(),
            'avalanche_statistics': {
                'mean_size': np.mean(sizes),
                'max_size': max(sizes),
                'distribution': 'power_law' if fit.power_law.xmin else 'exponential'
            }
        }
    
    def _estimate_temperature(self) -> float:
        """估计系统温度（偏离临界态的程度）"""
        # 高温度=高活动性=远离临界
        return np.mean(self.grid)
    
    def _distance_to_critical(self) -> float:
        """距离临界态的距离"""
        # 临界态：平均斜率接近阈值的一半
        avg_slope = np.mean(self.grid)
        optimal = self.threshold / 2
        return abs(avg_slope - optimal)

class EdgeOfChaosDetector:
    """混沌边缘检测器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.lyapunov_exponents = deque(maxlen=100)
        
    def calculate_lyapunov(self, 
                           time_series: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算最大Lyapunov指数
        
        λ > 0: 混沌（敏感依赖初始条件）
        λ = 0: 临界（边缘）
        λ < 0: 稳定（吸引子）
        """
        # 简化：使用Wolf算法近似
        n = len(time_series)
        if n < 10:
            return 0.0
        
        # 找到最近邻并追踪分离
        divergence = []
        for i in range(n - 10):
            # 找到最近邻
            distances = [abs(time_series[i] - time_series[j]) 
                        for j in range(n) if j != i]
            if distances:
                nearest_dist = min(distances)
                # 简化计算
                divergence.append(np.log(nearest_dist + 1e-10))
        
        if divergence:
            return np.mean(divergence) / 10  # 归一化
        
        return 0.0
    
    def detect_noise_type(self, 
                          signal: np.ndarray) -> str:
        """
        检测信号噪声类型（1/f分析）
        
        - 白噪声（flat spectrum）：过于无序
        - 1/f噪声（pink noise）：混沌边缘（健康）
        - 布朗噪声（1/f^2）：过于相关（僵化）
        """
        # FFT分析功率谱
        fft = np.fft.fft(signal)
        power_spectrum = np.abs(fft)**2
        
        # 拟合功率谱斜率
        freqs = np.fft.fftfreq(len(signal))
        # 只取正频率
        mask = freqs > 0
        log_freq = np.log(freqs[mask])
        log_power = np.log(power_spectrum[mask] + 1e-10)
        
        # 线性拟合
        slope = np.polyfit(log_freq, log_power, 1)[0]
        
        if -1.5 < slope < -0.5:
            return "PINK_NOISE_CHAOS_EDGE"  # 健康
        elif slope > -0.5:
            return "WHITE_NOISE_DISORDERED"  # 混乱
        else:
            return "BROWN_NOISE_RIGID"  # 僵化
    
    def recommend_tuning(self, 
                         current_state: str,
                         lyapunov: float) -> str:
        """推荐调节策略"""
        if current_state == "WHITE_NOISE_DISORDERED":
            return "增加结构（建立常规沟通机制，降低混沌度）"
        elif current_state == "BROWN_NOISE_RIGID":
            return "引入扰动（打破例行公事，增加不可预测性）"
        elif abs(lyapunov) < 0.1:
            return "维持当前（处于最佳临界状态）"
        else:
            return "微调观察（接近临界但需监测）"

class PowerLawOptimizer:
    """幂律优化器：管理不可预测的极端事件"""
    
    def calculate_tail_risk(self, 
                           event_sizes: List[int],
                           threshold: int = 10) -> float:
        """
        计算尾部风险（黑天鹅事件概率）
        
        使用极值理论（EVT）
        """
        if not event_sizes:
            return 0.0
        
        # 超过阈值的极端事件
        extremes = [s for s in event_sizes if s > threshold]
        
        # 使用广义帕累托分布拟合
        if len(extremes) < 5:
            return len(extremes) / len(event_sizes)
        
        # 简化：计算超过阈值的概率
        return len(extremes) / len(event_sizes)
    
    def optimize_preparation(self,
                             risk_level: float,
                             resources: float) -> Dict:
        """
        优化黑天鹅事件准备
        
        幂律世界：准备应对规模而非具体事件
        """
        # 幂律分布的期望损失
        if risk_level > 0.1:
            return {
                'strategy': 'antifragile',
                'buffer_size': resources * 0.3,  # 30%冗余
                'response_flexibility': 'high',
                'prediction_attempt': 'abandoned'  # 幂律下无法预测具体时间
            }
        else:
            return {
                'strategy': 'robust',
                'buffer_size': resources * 0.1,
                'response_flexibility': 'medium'
            }

# 验证代码
def test_soc():
    """测试自组织临界性"""
    print("=== 自组织临界性验证 ===")
    
    # 沙堆模型
    sandpile = SandpileTrustModel(grid_size=10)
    
    # 持续添加应力
    avalanches = []
    for i in range(100):
        x, y = np.random.randint(0, 10, 2)
        event = sandpile.add_trust_stress((x, y), 1.0)
        if event.size > 0:
            avalanches.append(event)
    
    print(f"总崩塌次数: {len(avalanches)}")
    print(f"最大崩塌规模: {max((a.size for a in avalanches), default=0)}")
    
    # 分析临界性
    criticality = sandpile.analyze_criticality()
    print(f"幂律指数α: {criticality.get('power_law_alpha', 'N/A')}")
    
    # 混沌边缘检测
    detector = EdgeOfChaosDetector()
    
    # 生成测试信号
    pink_noise = np.cumsum(np.random.randn(1000))  # 近似1/f
    white_noise = np.random.randn(1000)
    
    lyapunov_pink = detector.calculate_lyapunov(pink_noise)
    noise_type = detector.detect_noise_type(pink_noise)
    
    print(f"\nLyapunov指数（粉红噪声）: {lyapunov_pink:.4f}")
    print(f"噪声类型: {noise_type}")
    
    recommendation = detector.recommend_tuning(noise_type, lyapunov_pink)
    print(f"调节建议: {recommendation}")
    
    # 验证：粉红噪声应被识别为混沌边缘
    assert "PINK" in noise_type or "EDGE" in noise_type
    
    print("✓ 自组织临界性验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_soc()

第二十六轮：语用学与言语行为理论——语言作为行动

26.1 第一性原理洞察

核心发现：语言不仅是信息的载体（语义学），更是行动的执行（语用学）。合伙人对话中的言外之力（Illocutionary Force）决定了信任的实际状态： - 断言（Assertive）：“我们没问题”（可能掩盖问题） - 指令（Directive）：“你必须听我的”（权力展示） - 承诺（Commissive）：“我保证下周完成”（信任投资） - 表达（Expressive）：“我很失望”（情感暴露） - 宣告（Declarative）：“我们散伙吧”（关系状态改变）

26.2 代码实现：言语行为分析器

# speech_act_analysis.py
# 言语行为分析：语用学维度

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import re

class IllocutionaryForce(Enum):
    """言外之力类型"""
    ASSERTIVE = "assertive"      # 断言（陈述事实）
    DIRECTIVE = "directive"      # 指令（试图使对方行动）
    COMMISSIVE = "commissive"    # 承诺（承担义务）
    EXPRESSIVE = "expressive"    # 表达（情感状态）
    DECLARATIVE = "declarative"  # 宣告（改变现实）
    VERDICTIVE = "verdictive"    # 评判（评估价值）

@dataclass
class SpeechAct:
    """言语行为"""
    utterance: str
    force: IllocutionaryForce
    propositional_content: str
    sincerity_conditions: Dict[str, bool]  # 真诚性条件
    felicity_conditions: Dict[str, bool]  # 恰当性条件
    perlocutionary_effect: str  # 言后之果（实际效果）

class SpeechActAnalyzer:
    """言语行为分析器"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.force_indicators = {
            IllocutionaryForce.ASSERTIVE: [
                '是', '不是', '事实', '确实', '实际上', '客观说'
            ],
            IllocutionaryForce.DIRECTIVE: [
                '必须', '应该', '需要', '请', '要求', '命令', '建议'
            ],
            IllocutionaryForce.COMMISSIVE: [
                '保证', '承诺', '一定', '会', '负责', '包在我身上', '我发誓'
            ],
            IllocutionaryForce.EXPRESSIVE: [
                '感到', '觉得', '失望', '高兴', '愤怒', '抱歉', '感谢', '担心'
            ],
            IllocutionaryForce.DECLARATIVE: [
                '解散', '分手', '合并', '成立', '任命', '开除', '散伙', '离婚'
            ]
        }
        
    def analyze(self, utterance: str, context: Dict) -> SpeechAct:
        """
        分析言语行为
        
        步骤：
        1. 识别言外之力（Force）
        2. 提取命题内容
        3. 验证真诚性条件（说话者是否真心）
        4. 验证恰当性条件（是否有权说这话）
        5. 推断言后之果（对听者的影响）
        """
        # 1. 识别言外之力
        force = self._identify_force(utterance)
        
        # 2. 提取命题内容（简化：去除force标记词）
        content = self._extract_content(utterance, force)
        
        # 3. 验证真诚性（基于上下文和生理信号）
        sincerity = self._check_sincerity(utterance, context, force)
        
        # 4. 验证恰当性（基于说话者角色和场合）
        felicity = self._check_felicity(utterance, context, force)
        
        # 5. 推断效果
        effect = self._infer_perlocutionary_effect(force, sincerity, context)
        
        return SpeechAct(
            utterance=utterance,
            force=force,
            propositional_content=content,
            sincerity_conditions=sincerity,
            felicity_conditions=felicity,
            perlocutionary_effect=effect
        )
    
    def _identify_force(self, text: str) -> IllocutionaryForce:
        """识别言外之力"""
        text = text.lower()
        scores = {}
        
        for force, indicators in self.force_indicators.items():
            score = sum(1 for ind in indicators if ind in text)
            scores[force] = score
        
        # 选择最高分
        if scores:
            best = max(scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])
            if best[1] > 0:
                return best[0]
        
        return IllocutionaryForce.ASSERTIVE  # 默认
    
    def _extract_content(self, text: str, force: IllocutionaryForce) -> str:
        """提取命题内容"""
        # 去除force标记词
        content = text
        for ind in self.force_indicators.get(force, []):
            content = content.replace(ind, '')
        return content.strip()
    
    def _check_sincerity(self, 
                         text: str, 
                         context: Dict,
                         force: IllocutionaryForce) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """检查真诚性条件"""
        checks = {}
        
        # 断言的真诚性：相信所言为真
        if force == IllocutionaryForce.ASSERTIVE:
            # 检查与过去行为的一致性
            consistency = context.get('past_statements_consistency', 0.5)
            checks['believes_true'] = consistency > 0.7
        
        # 承诺的真诚性：有意履行
        elif force == IllocutionaryForce.COMMISSIVE:
            # 检查历史承诺履行率
            fulfillment_rate = context.get('promise_fulfillment_history', 0.5)
            checks['intends_to_fulfill'] = fulfillment_rate > 0.6
        
        # 表达的真诚性：确实有所表达的情感
        elif force == IllocutionaryForce.EXPRESSIVE:
            # 检查生理信号匹配（如果有）
            physiological_match = context.get('physiological_arousal_match', True)
            checks['feels_emotion'] = physiological_match
        
        return checks
    
    def _check_felicity(self,
                        text: str,
                        context: Dict,
                        force: IllocutionaryForce) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """检查恰当性条件（Felicity Conditions）"""
        checks = {}
        
        # 宣告的恰当性：必须有权威
        if force == IllocutionaryForce.DECLARATIVE:
            has_authority = context.get('speaker_authority', False)
            checks['has_authority'] = has_authority
        
        # 指令的恰当性：听者有能力执行
        elif force == IllocutionaryForce.DIRECTIVE:
            hearer_ability = context.get('hearer_capability', True)
            checks['hearer_able'] = hearer_ability
        
        return checks
    
    def _infer_perlocutionary_effect(self,
                                      force: IllocutionaryForce,
                                      sincerity: Dict,
                                      context: Dict) -> str:
        """推断言后之果（对听者的实际影响）"""
        if force == IllocutionaryForce.COMMISSIVE:
            if sincerity.get('intends_to_fulfill', False):
                return "trust_increase"  # 承诺真诚->信任增加
            else:
                return "trust_decrease"  # 虚假承诺->信任降低
        
        elif force == IllocutionaryForce.DIRECTIVE:
            if not sincerity.get('hearer_able', True):
                return "frustration"  # 无法执行的指令->挫败
            else:
                return "compliance_or_resistance"
        
        elif force == IllocutionaryForce.DECLARATIVE:
            return "reality_change"  # 现实改变
        
        return "information_received"

class ConversationGameAnalyzer:
    """对话博弈分析：语言作为策略行为"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.payoff_matrix = {
            'cooperative': {'cooperative': (3, 3), 'defensive': (0, 5)},
            'defensive': {'cooperative': (5, 0), 'defensive': (1, 1)}
        }
    
    def analyze_conversation_strategy(self,
                                      dialogue_history: List[SpeechAct]) -> Dict:
        """
        分析对话策略：合作vs防御
        
        类似囚徒困境的重复博弈
        """
        strategies = []
        
        for i, act in enumerate(dialogue_history):
            if act.force in [IllocutionaryForce.ASSERTIVE, IllocutionaryForce.EXPRESSIVE]:
                strategy = 'cooperative' if act.sincerity_conditions.get('believes_true', True) else 'defensive'
            elif act.force == IllocutionaryForce.DIRECTIVE:
                strategy = 'defensive' if '必须' in act.utterance else 'cooperative'
            else:
                strategy = 'neutral'
            
            strategies.append(strategy)
        
        # 检测模式
        tit_for_tat = self._detect_tit_for_tat(strategies)
        spiral = self._detect_escalation_spiral(strategies)
        
        return {
            'strategy_sequence': strategies,
            'is_tit_for_tat': tit_for_tat,  # 以牙还牙（稳定）
            'is_escalation_spiral': spiral,  # 升级螺旋（危险）
            'cooperation_rate': strategies.count('cooperative') / len(strategies)
        }
    
    def _detect_tit_for_tat(self, strategies: List[str]) -> bool:
        """检测以牙还牙策略（镜像回应）"""
        if len(strategies) < 4:
            return False
        
        # 检查是否镜像对方上一步
        mirroring = sum(1 for i in range(1, len(strategies)) 
                      if strategies[i] == strategies[i-1])
        return mirroring / (len(strategies) - 1) > 0.7
    
    def _detect_escalation_spiral(self, strategies: List[str]) -> bool:
        """检测升级螺旋（双方越来越防御）"""
        if len(strategies) < 4:
            return False
        
        # 检测防御性递增
        first_half = strategies[:len(strategies)//2].count('defensive')
        second_half = strategies[len(strategies)//2:].count('defensive')
        return second_half > first_half * 1.5

class PragmaticPresuppositionAnalyzer:
    """预设分析：未明言的假设"""
    
    def extract_presuppositions(self, text: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        提取话语中的预设（被视为理所当然的背景假设）
        
        例如："你停止打你合伙人了吗？"预设"你曾经打过"
        """
        presuppositions = []
        
        # 事实性动词预设
        factive_verbs = ['后悔', '知道', '意识到', '发现']
        for verb in factive_verbs:
            if verb in text:
                # 提取宾语从句作为预设
                presuppositions.append({
                    'trigger': verb,
                    'presupposed_content': f'[{verb}]的内容为真',
                    'type': 'factive'
                })
        
        # 限定词预设
        definites = re.findall(r'那个(\w+)', text)
        for definite in definites:
            presuppositions.append({
                'trigger': f'那个{definite}',
                'presupposed_content': f'{definite}存在且唯一',
                'type': 'existential'
            })
        
        # 迭代词预设（又、再）
        if '又' in text or '再' in text:
            presuppositions.append({
                'trigger': '又/再',
                'presupposed_content': '此事曾发生过',
                'type': 'iterative'
            })
        
        return presuppositions

# 验证代码
def test_speech_acts():
    """测试言语行为分析"""
    print("=== 言语行为分析验证 ===")
    
    analyzer = SpeechActAnalyzer()
    
    # 测试用例
    test_cases = [
        "我保证下周完成产品上线",  # 承诺
        "你必须听我的技术方案",     # 指令
        "我感到你对我不够尊重",     # 表达
        "我们散伙吧",               # 宣告
        "事实上我们的方向是对的"    # 断言
    ]
    
    for text in test_cases:
        context = {
            'past_statements_consistency': 0.8,
            'promise_fulfillment_history': 0.9,
            'speaker_authority': True
        }
        
        act = analyzer.analyze(text, context)
        print(f"\n话语: {text}")
        print(f"  言外之力: {act.force.value}")
        print(f"  命题内容: {act.propositional_content}")
        print(f"  真诚性: {act.sincerity_conditions}")
        print(f"  言后之果: {act.perlocutionary_effect}")
    
    # 对话博弈分析
    dialogue = [
        SpeechAct("我们一起努力", IllocutionaryForce.COMMISSIVE, 
                 "合作", {'intends_to_fulfill': True}, {}, ""),
        SpeechAct("你总是拖延", IllocutionaryForce.EXPRESSIVE,
                 "不满", {'feels_emotion': True}, {}, ""),
        SpeechAct("你必须按时交付", IllocutionaryForce.DIRECTIVE,
                 "按时", {}, {'hearer_able': True}, ""),
    ]
    
    game_analyzer = ConversationGameAnalyzer()
    strategy = game_analyzer.analyze_conversation_strategy(dialogue)
    print(f"\n对话策略分析:")
    print(f"  合作率: {strategy['cooperation_rate']:.1%}")
    print(f"  以牙还牙模式: {strategy['is_tit_for_tat']}")
    
    # 预设分析
    presuppo_analyzer = PragmaticPresuppositionAnalyzer()
    press = presuppo_analyzer.extract_presuppositions("我后悔那个决定，又犯了同样的错")
    print(f"\n预设分析: {len(press)}个预设")
    for p in press:
        print(f"  - {p['trigger']}: {p['presupposed_content']}")
    
    print("✓ 言语行为分析验证通过")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_speech_acts()
